add TypeScript types matching API design spec\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 4: API Client Layer\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `web/lib/api/client.ts`\n\n**Step 1: Write typed fetch wrappers**\n\n```ts\n// web/lib/api/client.ts\nimport type {\n  ApiResponse,\n  Project,\n  ProjectInput,\n  Release,\n  PipelineStatus,\n  Source,\n  SourceInput,\n  Subscription,\n  SubscriptionInput,\n  NotificationChannel,\n  ChannelInput,\n  Provider,\n  HealthStatus,\n  Stats,\n} from \"./types\";\n\nconst BASE = \"/api/v1\";\n\nasync function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {\n  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {\n    headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", ...init?.headers },\n    ...init,\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);\n    throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? `Request failed: ${res.status}`);\n  }\n  return res.json();\n}\n\n// --- Projects ---\n\nexport const projects = {\n  list: (page = 1, perPage = 25) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Project[]>>(`/projects?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),\n  get: (id: number) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`),\n  create: (input: ProjectInput) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(\"/projects\", {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      body: JSON.stringify(input),\n    }),\n  update: (id: number, input: ProjectInput) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`, {\n      method: \"PUT\",\n      body: JSON.stringify(input),\n    }),\n  delete: (id: number) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/projects/${id}`, { method: \"DELETE\" }),\n};\n\n// --- Releases ---\n\nexport interface ListReleasesParams {\n  project_id?: number;\n  source_id?: number;\n  pre_release?: boolean;\n  page?: number;\n  per_page?: number;\n  sort?: string;\n  order?: \"asc\" | \"desc\";\n}\n\nexport const releases = {\n  list: (params: ListReleasesParams = {}) => {\n    const qs = new URLSearchParams();\n    if (params.project_id) qs.set(\"project_id\", String(params.project_id));\n    if (params.source_id) qs.set(\"source_id\", String(params.source_id));\n    if (params.pre_release !== undefined) qs.set(\"pre_release\", String(params.pre_release));\n    qs.set(\"page\", String(params.page ?? 1));\n    qs.set(\"per_page\", String(params.per_page ?? 25));\n    if (params.sort) qs.set(\"sort\", params.sort);\n    if (params.order) qs.set(\"order\", params.order);\n    return request<ApiResponse<Release[]>>(`/releases?${qs}`);\n  },\n  get: (id: string) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Release>>(`/releases/${id}`),\n  pipeline: (id: string) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<PipelineStatus>>(`/releases/${id}/pipeline`),\n  notes: (id: string) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<string>>(`/releases/${id}/notes`),\n};\n\n// --- Sources ---\n\nexport const sources = {\n  list: () => request<ApiResponse<Source[]>>(\"/sources\"),\n  get: (id: number) => request<ApiResponse<Source>>(`/sources/${id}`),\n  create: (input: SourceInput) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Source>>(\"/sources\", {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      body: JSON.stringify(input),\n    }),\n  update: (id: number, input: SourceInput) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Source>>(`/sources/${id}`, {\n      method: \"PUT\",\n      body: JSON.stringify(input),\n    }),\n  delete: (id: number) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/sources/${id}`, { method: \"DELETE\" }),\n  latestRelease: (id: number) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Release>>(`/sources/${id}/latest-release`),\n};\n\n// --- Subscriptions ---\n\nexport const subscriptions = {\n  list: () => request<ApiResponse<Subscription[]>>(\"/subscriptions\"),\n  get: (id: number) => request<ApiResponse<Subscription>>(`/subscriptions/${id}`),\n  create: (input: SubscriptionInput) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Subscription>>(\"/subscriptions\", {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      body: JSON.stringify(input),\n    }),\n  update: (id: number, input: SubscriptionInput) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<Subscription>>(`/subscriptions/${id}`, {\n      method: \"PUT\",\n      body: JSON.stringify(input),\n    }),\n  delete: (id: number) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/subscriptions/${id}`, { method: \"DELETE\" }),\n};\n\n// --- Notification Channels ---\n\nexport const channels = {\n  list: () => request<ApiResponse<NotificationChannel[]>>(\"/channels\"),\n  get: (id: number) => request<ApiResponse<NotificationChannel>>(`/channels/${id}`),\n  create: (input: ChannelInput) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<NotificationChannel>>(\"/channels\", {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      body: JSON.stringify(input),\n    }),\n  update: (id: number, input: ChannelInput) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<NotificationChannel>>(`/channels/${id}`, {\n      method: \"PUT\",\n      body: JSON.stringify(input),\n    }),\n  delete: (id: number) =>\n    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/channels/${id}`, { method: \"DELETE\" }),\n};\n\n// --- System ---\n\nexport const system = {\n  health: () => request<HealthStatus>(\"/health\"),\n  stats: () => request<ApiResponse<Stats>>(\"/stats\"),\n  providers: () => request<ApiResponse<Provider[]>>(\"/providers\"),\n};\n```\n\n**Step 2: Verify TypeScript compiles**\n\n```bash\ncd import(\"./mock/browser\").then(({ worker }) => {\n      worker.start({ onUnhandledRequest: \"bypass\" }).then(() => setReady(true));\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  if (!ready) return null;\n  return \"...\" : item.value}</div>\n          </CardContent>\n        </Card>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Write the recent releases component**\n\n```tsx\n// web/components/dashboard/recent-releases.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport { releases as releasesApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Badge } from \"@/components/ui/badge\";\n\nconst statusColors: Record<string, string> = {\n  completed: \"bg-green-100 text-green-800\",\n  running: \"bg-blue-100 text-blue-800\",\n  available: \"bg-gray-100 text-gray-800\",\n  retry: \"bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800\",\n  discarded: \"bg-red-100 text-red-800\",\n};\n\nexport function RecentReleases() {\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(\"recent-releases\", () =>\n    releasesApi.list({ per_page: 8, order: \"desc\" })\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <Card>\n      <CardHeader className=\"flex flex-row items-center justify-between\">\n        <CardTitle>Recent Releases</CardTitle>\n        <Link href=\"/releases\" className=\"text-sm text-primary hover:underline\">\n          View all\n        </Link>\n      </CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        {isLoading ? (\n          <div className=\"py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n            {data?.data.map((release) => (\n              <Link\n                key={release.id}\n                href={`/releases/${release.id}`}\n                className=\"flex items-center justify-between rounded-md border p-3 transition-colors hover:bg-muted/50\"\n              >\n                <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n                  <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                    <span Create: `web/app/projects/[id]/edit/page.tsx`\n\n**Step 1: Write the reusable project form**\n\n```tsx\n// web/components/projects/project-form.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Input } from \"@/components/ui/input\";\nimport { Label } from \"@/components/ui/label\";\nimport { Textarea } from \"@/components/ui/textarea\";\nimport { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport type { Project, ProjectInput } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\n\ninterface ProjectFormProps {\n  initial?: Project;\n  onSubmit: (input: ProjectInput) => Promise<void>;\n  title: string;\n}\n\nexport function ProjectForm({ initial, onSubmit, title }: ProjectFormProps) {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name ?? \"\");\n  const [description, setDescription] = useState(initial?.description ?? \"\");\n  const [url, setUrl] = useState(initial?.url ?? \"\");\n  const [pipelineConfig, setPipelineConfig] = useState(\n    JSON.stringify(initial?.pipeline_config ?? { changelog_summarizer: {}, urgency_scorer: {} }, null, 2)\n  );\n  const [error, setError] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError(\"\");\n    let parsedConfig: Record<string, unknown>;\n    try {\n      parsedConfig = JSON.parse(pipelineConfig);\n    } catch {\n      setError(\"Pipeline config must be valid JSON\");\n      return;\n    }\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await onSubmit({ name, description, url, pipeline_config: parsedConfig });\n      router.push(\"/projects\");\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Failed to save\");\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Card className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl\">\n      <CardHeader>\n        <CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle>\n      </CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n          {error && <div className=\"rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700\">{error}</div>}\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"name\">Name</Label>\n            <Input id=\"name\" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} required />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"description\">Description</Label>\n            <Textarea id=\"description\" value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} rows={2} />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"url\">URL</Label>\n            <Input id=\"url\" type=\"url\" value={url} onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)} />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"pipeline_config\">Pipeline Config (JSON)</Label>\n            <Textarea\n              id=\"pipeline_config\"\n              value={pipelineConfig}\n              onChange={(e) => setPipelineConfig(e.target.value)}\n              rows={8}\n              className=\"font-mono text-sm\"\n            />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex justify-end gap-2\">\n            <Button type=\"button\" variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => router.back()}>Cancel</Button>\n            <Button type=\"submit\" disabled={saving}>{saving ? \"Saving...\" : \"Save\"}</Button>\n          </div>\n        </form>\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Write the create project text-yellow-800\",\n  discarded: \"bg-red-100 text-red-800\",\n};\n\nexport default function ReleasesPage() {\n  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);\n  const [projectFilter, setProjectFilter] = params.pre_release = preReleaseFilter === \"true\";\n\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(\n    [\"releases\", page, projectFilter, preReleaseFilter],\n    () => releasesApi.list(params)\n  );\n\n  const total = data?.meta.total ?? 0;\n  const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / 15);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <div>\n        <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">All Releases</h2>\n        <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">Browse releases ingested from all sources.</p>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Filters */}\n      <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n        <Select value={projectFilter} onValueChange={(v) => { setProjectFilter(v); setPage(1); }}>\n          <SelectTrigger className=\"w-48\">\n            <SelectValue placeholder=\"All Projects\" />\n          </SelectTrigger>\n          <SelectContent>\n            <SelectItem value=\"all\">All Projects</SelectItem>\n            {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (\n              <SelectItem key={p.id} value={String(p.id)}>{p.name}</SelectItem>\n            ))}\n          </SelectContent>\n        </Select>\n\n        <Select value={preReleaseFilter} onValueChange={(v) => { setPreReleaseFilter(v); setPage(1); }}>\n          <SelectTrigger className=\"w-40\">\n            <SelectValue placeholder=\"All Types\" />\n          </SelectTrigger>\n          <SelectContent>\n            <SelectItem value=\"all\">All Types</SelectItem>\n            <SelectItem value=\"false\">Stable Only</SelectItem>\n            <SelectItem value=\"true\">Pre-release Only</SelectItem>\n          </SelectContent>\n        </Select>\n      </div>\n\n      <Card>\n        <CardContent className=\"p-0\">\n          {isLoading ? (\n            <div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>\n          ) : (\n            <Table>\n              <TableHeader>\n                <TableRow>\n                  <TableHead>Version</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Project</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Source</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Pipeline</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Date</TableHead>\n                </TableRow>\n              </TableHeader>\n              <TableBody>\n                {data?.data.map((release) => (\n                  <TableRow key={release.id}>\n                    <TableCell>\n                      <Link\n                        href={`/releases/${release.id}`}\n                        className=\"flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-sm text-primary hover:underline\"\n                      >\n                        {release.raw_version}\n                        {release.is_pre_release && (\n                          <Badge variant=\"outline\" className=\"text-xs\">pre</Badge>\n                        )}\n                      </Link>\n                    </TableCell>\n                    <TableCell>\n                      <Link href={`/projects/${release.project_id}`} className=\"hover:underline\">\n                        {release.project_name}\n                      </Link>\n                    </TableCell>\n                    <TableCell>\n                      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5\">\n                        <Badge variant=\"outline\" className=\"text-xs\">{release.source_type}</Badge>\n                        <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{release.repository}</span>\n                      </div>\n                    </TableCell>\n                    <TableCell>\n                      <Badge className={statusColors[release.pipeline_status] ?? \"\"}>\n                        {release.pipeline_status}\n                      </Badge>\n                    </TableCell>\n                    <TableCell className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n                      {new Date(release.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}\n                    </TableCell>\n                  </TableRow>\n                ))}\n              </TableBody>\n            </Table>\n          )}\n        </CardContent>\n      </Card>\n\n      {/* Pagination */}\n      {totalPages > 1 && (\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <span className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">{total} releases</span>\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <Button variant=\"outline\" size=\"sm\" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}>\n              <ChevronLeft className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n            </Button>\n            <span className=\"text-sm\">Page {page} of {totalPages}</span>\n            <Button variant=\"outline\" size=\"sm\" disabled={page >= totalPages} onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}>\n              <ChevronRight className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n            </Button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Verify releases page with filters**\n\nVisit http://localhost:3000/releases — should see table with all releases. web/components/releases/pipeline-status.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Badge } from Assessor\",\n  adoption_tracker: \"Adoption Tracker\",\n  changelog_summarizer: \"Changelog Summarizer\",\n  urgency_scorer: \"Urgency Scorer\",\n  validation_trigger: \"Validation Trigger\",\n};\n\nconst stateIcons: Record<string, React.ReactNode> = {\n  completed: <CheckCircle className=\"h-5 w-5 text-green-600\" />,\n  running: <Loader2 className=\"h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-blue-600\" />,\n  discarded: <XCircle className=\"h-5 w-5 text-red-600\" />,\n  retry: <AlertCircle className=\"h-5 w-5 text-yellow-600\" />,\n  available: <Circle className=\"h-5 w-5 text-gray-400\" />,\n};\n\ninterface PipelineVisualizationProps {\n  pipeline: PipelineStatus;\n}\n\nexport function PipelineVisualization({ pipeline }: PipelineVisualizationProps) {\n  const completedNodes = Object.keys(pipeline.node_results);\n\n  return (\n    <Card>\n      <CardHeader className=\"flex flex-row items-center justify-between\">\n        <CardTitle className=\"text-base\">Pipeline Status</CardTitle>\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          {stateIcons[pipeline.state]}\n          <Badge\n            className={\n              pipeline.state === \"completed\" ? \"bg-green-100 text-green-800\" :\n              pipeline.state === \"running\" ? \"bg-blue-100 text-blue-800\" :\n              pipeline.state === \"discarded\" ? \"bg-red-100 text-red-800\" :\n              \"bg-gray-100 text-gray-800\"\n            }\n          >\n            {pipeline.state}\n          </Badge>\n          {pipeline.current_node && (\n            <span className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n              Currently: {nodeLabels[pipeline.current_node] ?? pipeline.current_node}\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n          {completedNodes.map((nodeName) => {\n            const result = pipeline.node_results[nodeName] as Record<string, unknown>;\n            return (\n              <div key={nodeName} className=\"rounded-md border p-4\">\n                <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n                  <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                    <CheckCircle className=\"h-4 w-4 text-green-600\" />\n                    <span className=\"font-medium text-sm\">\n                      {nodeLabels[nodeName] ?? nodeName}\n                    </span>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n                <div className=\"mt-2 grid gap-2\">\n                  {Object.entries(result).map(([key, value]) => (\n                    <div key={key} className=\"flex gap-2 text-sm\">\n                      <span className=\"font-medium text-muted-foreground min-w-[140px]\">\n                        {key.replace(/_/g, \" \")}:\n                      </span>\n                      <span>\n                        {typeof value === \"object\"\n                          ? JSON.stringify(value)\n                          : String(value)}\n                      </span>\n                    </div>\n                  ))}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            );\n          })}\n\n          {pipeline.current_node && !completedNodes.includes(pipeline.current_node) && (\n            <div className=\"rounded-md border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-4\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <Loader2 className=\"h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-blue-600\" />\n                <span className=\"font-medium text-sm text-blue-800\">\n                  {nodeLabels[pipeline.current_node] ?? pipeline.current_node}\n                </span>\n                <span className=\"text-xs text-blue-600\">Processing...</span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {pipeline.attempt > 1 && (\n          <div className=\"mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n            Attempt {pipeline.attempt} of 3\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Write the release detail <Button variant=\"ghost\" size=\"sm\">\n          <ArrowLeft className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" /> Back to Releases\n        </Button>\n      </Link>\n\n      {/* Release Header */}\n      <div>\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n          <h2 className=\"text-2xl font-bold font-mono\">{release.raw_version}</h2>\n          {release.is_pre_release && <Badge variant=\"outline\">pre-release</Badge>}\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground\">\n          <Link href={`/projects/${release.project_id}`} className=\"text-primary hover:underline\">\n            {release.project_name}\n          </Link>\n          <span>&middot;</span>\n          <Badge variant=\"outline\">{release.source_type}</Badge>\n          <span className=\"font-mono text-sm\">{release.repository}</span>\n          <span>&middot;</span>\n          <span>{new Date(release.created_at).toLocaleString()}</span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-3\">\n        {/* Left column: Metadata + Notes */}\n        <div className=\"space-y-6 lg:col-span-1\">\n          <Card>\n            <CardHeader><CardTitle className=\"text-base\">Version Details</CardTitle></CardHeader>\n            <CardContent className=\"space-y-2 text-sm\">\n              <div className=\"flex justify-between\">\n                <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">Semantic</span>\n                <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">Source Type</span>\n                <span>{release.source_type}</span>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex justify-between\">\n                <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">Repository</span>\n                <span className=\"font-mono text-xs\">{release.repository}</span>\n              </div>\n              {Object.entries(release.metadata).map(([k, v]) => (\n                <div key={k} className=\"flex justify-between\">\n                  <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">{k}</span>\n                  <span className=\"font-mono text-xs truncate max-w-[200px]\">{v}</span>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </CardContent>\n          </Card>\n\n          {notesData?.data && (\n            <Card>\n              <CardHeader><CardTitle className=\"text-base\">Release Notes</CardTitle></CardHeader>\n              <CardContent>\n                <div className=\"prose prose-sm max-w-none\">\n                  <pre className=\"whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm\">{notesData.data}</pre>\n                </div>\n              </CardContent>\n            </Card>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Right column: Pipeline */}\n        <div className=\"lg:col-span-2\">\n          {loadingPipeline ? (\n            <div className=\"py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading pipeline...</div>\n          ) : pipelineData?.data ? (\n            <PipelineVisualization pipeline={pipelineData.data} />\n          ) : (\n            <Card>\n              <CardContent className=\"py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground\">\n form component**\n\n```tsx\n// web/components/sources/source-form.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport { projects as projectsApi, system } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Input } from \"@/components/ui/input\";\nimport { Label } from \"@/components/ui/label\";\nimport { Switch } from \"@/components/ui/switch\";\nimport { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport {\n  Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue,\n} from \"@/components/ui/select\";\nimport type { Source, SourceInput } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\n\ninterface SourceFormProps {\n  initial?: Source;\n  defaultProjectId?: number;\n  onSubmit: (input: SourceInput) => Promise<void>;\n  title: string;\n}\n\nexport function SourceForm({ initial, defaultProjectId, onSubmit, title }: SourceFormProps) {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState(\"\");\n  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState(String(initial?.project_id ?? defaultProjectId ?? \"\"));\n  const [type, setType] = useState(initial?.type ?? \"dockerhub\");\n  const [repository, setRepository] = useState(initial?.repository ?? \"\");\n  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState(String(initial?.poll_interval_seconds ?? 300));\n  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(initial?.enabled ?? true);\n  const [excludeRegexp, setExcludeRegexp] = useState(initial?.exclude_version_regexp ?? \"\");\n  const [excludePrereleases, setExcludePrereleases] = useState(initial?.exclude_prereleases ?? false);\n\n  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR(\"projects-for-form\", () => projectsApi.list());\n  const { data: providersData } = useSWR(\"providers\", () => system.providers());\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError(\"\");\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await onSubmit({\n        project_id: Number(projectId),\n        type,\n        repository,\n        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval),\n        enabled,\n        exclude_version_regexp: excludeRegexp || undefined,\n        exclude_prereleases: excludePrereleases || undefined,\n      });\n      router.push(\"/sources\");\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Failed to save\");\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Card className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl\">\n      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n          {error && <div className=\"rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700\">{error}</div>}\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Project</Label>\n            <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={setProjectId} required>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder=\"Select project\" /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (\n                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={String(p.id)}>{p.name}</SelectItem>\n                ))}\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Source Type</Label>\n            <Select value={type} onValueChange={setType}>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n                {(providersData?.data ?? [{ type: \"dockerhub\", name: \"Docker Hub\" }, { type: \"github\", name: \"GitHub\" }]).map((p) => (\n                  <SelectItem key={p.type} value={p.type}>{p.name}</SelectItem>\n                ))}\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"repository\">Repository</Label>\n            <Input id=\"repository\" value={repository} onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)} placeholder=\"e.g. library/golang or ethereum/go-ethereum\" required />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"poll_interval\">Poll Interval (seconds)</Label>\n            <Input id=\"poll_interval\" type=\"number\" min={60} value={pollInterval} onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)} />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"exclude_regexp\">Exclude Version Pattern (regex)</Label>\n            <Input id=\"exclude_regexp\" value={excludeRegexp} onChange={(e) => setExcludeRegexp(e.target.value)} placeholder=\"e.g. -(alpha|beta|nightly)\" />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n            <Switch checked={excludePrereleases} onCheckedChange={setExcludePrereleases} />\n            <Label>Exclude pre-releases</Label>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n            <Switch checked={enabled} onCheckedChange={setEnabled} />\n            <Label>Enabled</Label>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex justify-end gap-2\">\n            <Button type=\"button\" variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => router.back()}>Cancel</Button>\n            <Button type=\"submit\" disabled={saving}>{saving ? \"Saving...\" : \"Save\"}</Button>\n          </div>\n        </form>\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Write the sources list page**\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/sources/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport { sources as sourcesApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Card, CardContent } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Badge } from \"@/components/ui/badge\";\nimport {\n  Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow,\n} from \"@/components/ui/table\";\nimport { Plus } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nexport default function SourcesPage() {\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(\"sources\", () => sourcesApi.list());\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <div>\n          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">All Sources</h2>\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">Ingestion sources that poll upstream registries.</p>\n        </div>\n        <Link href=\"/sources/new\">\n          <Button><Plus className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />Add Source</Button>\n        </Link>\n      </div>\n      <Card>\n        <CardContent className=\"p-0\">\n          {isLoading ? (\n            <div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>\n          ) : (\n            <Table>\n              <TableHeader>\n                <TableRow>\n                  <TableHead>Repository</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Type</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Poll Interval</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Last Polled</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Status</TableHead>\n                </TableRow>\n              </TableHeader>\n              <TableBody>\n                {data?.data.map((source) => (\n                  <TableRow key={source.id}>\n                    <TableCell>\n                      <Link href={`/sources/${source.id}/edit`} className=\"font-mono text-sm text-primary hover:underline\">\n                        {source.repository}\n                      </Link>\n                    </TableCell>\n                    <TableCell><Badge variant=\"outline\">{source.type}</Badge></TableCell>\n                    <TableCell className=\"text-sm\">{source.poll_interval_seconds}s</TableCell>\n                    <TableCell className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n                      {source.last_polled_at ? new Date(source.last_polled_at).toLocaleString() : \"Never\"}\n                    </TableCell>\n                    <TableCell>\n                      {source.last_error ? (\n                        <Badge variant=\"destructive\" className=\"text-xs\">{source.last_error}</Badge>\n                      ) : (\n                        <Badge variant={source.enabled ? \"default\" : \"secondary\"}>\n                          {source.enabled ? \"active\" : \"disabled\"}\n                        </Badge>\n                      )}\n                    </TableCell>\n                  </TableRow>\n                ))}\n              </TableBody>\n            </Table>\n          )}\n        </CardContent>\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 3: Write create and edit source pages**\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/sources/new/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useSearchParams } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { sources as sourcesApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { SourceForm } from \"@/components/sources/source-form\";\n\nexport default function NewSourcePage() {\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const projectId = searchParams.get(\"project_id\");\n\n  return (\n    <SourceForm\n      title=\"Add Source\"\n      defaultProjectId={projectId ? Number(projectId) : undefined}\n      onSubmit={async (input) => { await sourcesApi.create(input); }}\n    />\n  );\n}\n```\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/sources/[id]/edit/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { use } from \"react\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { sources as sourcesApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { SourceForm } from \"@/components/sources/source-form\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Trash2 } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nexport default function EditSourcePage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  const { id } = use(params);\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(`source-${id}`, () => sourcesApi.get(Number(id)));\n\n  const handleDelete = async () => {\n    if (!confirm(\"Delete this source?\")) return;\n    await sourcesApi.delete(Number(id));\n    router.push(\"/sources\");\n  };\n\n  if (isLoading) return <div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>;\n  if (!data?.data) return <div className=\"py-12 text-center\">Source not found</div>;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <SourceForm\n        title=\"Edit Source\"\n        initial={data.data}\n        onSubmit={async (input) => { await sourcesApi.update(Number(id), input); }}\n      />\n      <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl flex justify-end\">\n        <Button variant=\"destructive\" size=\"sm\" onClick={handleDelete}>\n          <Trash2 className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" /> Delete Source\n        </Button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 4: Verify sources pages**\n\n- Visit http://localhost:3000/sources — table with all 6 sources\n- Visit `web/app/subscriptions/[id]/edit/page.tsx`\n\n**Step 1: Write subscription form**\n\n```tsx\n// web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from useSWR(\"projects-for-sub\", () => projectsApi.list());\n  const { data: channelsData } = useSWR(\"channels-for-sub\", () => channelsApi.list());\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) channel_id: Number(channelId),\n        version_pattern: versionPattern || undefined,\n        frequency: frequency as SubscriptionInput[\"frequency\"],\n        enabled,\n      });\n      router.push(\"/subscriptions\");\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Failed to save\");\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Card className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl\">\n      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n          {error && <div className=\"rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700\">{error}</div>}\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Project</Label>\n            <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={setProjectId} required>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder=\"Select project\" /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (\n                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={String(p.id)}>{p.name}</SelectItem>\n                ))}\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Channel Type</Label>\n            <Select value={channelType} string }[]> = {\n  slack: [\n    { label: \"Webhook URL\", placeholder: \"https://hooks.slack.com/services/...\" },\n    { label: \"Channel\", placeholder: \"#releases\" },\n  ],\n  pagerduty: [\n    { label: \"Routing Key\", placeholder: \"R0xxxxx\" },\n  ],\n  webhook: [\n    { label: \"URL\", placeholder: \"https://your-service.com/api/releases\" },\n    { label: \"Headers\", placeholder: function ChannelForm({ initial, onSubmit, title }: ChannelFormProps) {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState(\"\");\n  const [type, setType] = useState(initial?.type ?? \"slack\");\n  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name ?? \"\");\n  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(initial?.enabled ?? true);\n  const [config, setConfig] = useState<Record<string, string>>(initial?.config ?? {});\n\n  const fields = channelFields[type] ?? [];\n\n  const handleConfigChange = (label: string, value: string) => {\n    const key = label.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g, \"_\");\n    setConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));\n  };\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError(\"\");\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await onSubmit({ type, name, config, enabled });\n      router.push(\"/channels\");\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Failed to save\");\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Card className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl\">\n      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n          {error && <div className=\"rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700\">{error}</div>}\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"name\">Name</Label>\n            <Input id=\"name\" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder=\"e.g. Engineering Releases\" required />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Type</Label>\n            <Select value={type} onValueChange={(v) => { setType(v); setConfig({}); }}>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n                <SelectItem value=\"slack\">Slack</SelectItem>\n                <SelectItem value=\"pagerduty\">PagerDuty</SelectItem>\n                <SelectItem value=\"webhook\">Webhook</SelectItem>\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          {fields.map((field) => {\n            const key = field.label.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g, \"_\");\n            return (\n              <div key={key} className=\"space-y-2\">\n                <Label htmlFor={key}>{field.label}</Label>\n                <Input\n                  id={key}\n                  value={config[key] ?? \"\"}\n                  onChange={(e) => handleConfigChange(field.label, e.target.value)}\n                  placeholder={field.placeholder}\n                />\n              </div>\n            );\n          })}\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n            <Switch checked={enabled} onCheckedChange={setEnabled} />\n            <Label>Enabled</Label>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex justify-end gap-2\">\n            <Button type=\"button\" variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => router.back()}>Cancel</Button>\n            <Button type=\"submit\" disabled={saving}>{saving ? \"Saving...\" : \"Save\"}</Button>\n          </div>\n        </form>\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Write channels list page**\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/channels/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport { channels as channelsApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Card, CardContent } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Badge } from \"@/components/ui/badge\";\nimport {\n  Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow,\n} from \"@/components/ui/table\";\nimport { Plus } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nconst typeIcons: Record<string, string> = {\n  slack: \"Slack\",\n  pagerduty: \"PagerDuty\",\n  webhook: \"Webhook\",\n};\n\nexport default function ChannelsPage() {\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(\"channels\", () => channelsApi.list());\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <div>\n          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">Notification Channels</h2>\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">Output targets for release notifications.</p>\n        </div>\n        <Link href=\"/channels/new\">\n          <Button><Plus className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />Add Channel</Button>\n        </Link>\n      </div>\n      <Card>\n        <CardContent className=\"p-0\">\n          {isLoading ? Instead, we create a mock SSE emitter that simulates real-time events in development.\n\n**Step 1: Write the mock SSE emitter**\n\n```ts\n// web/lib/mock/sse.ts\nimport type { SSEEvent, SSEEventType } from \"../api/types\";\n\ntype SSEListener = (event: SSEEvent) => void;\n\nclass MockSSE {\n  private listeners: SSEListener[] = [];\n  private interval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;\n\n  subscribe(listener: SSEListener) {\n    this.listeners.push(listener);\n    if (!this.interval) this.startEmitting();\n    return () => {\n      this.listeners = this.listeners.filter((l) => l !== listener);\n      if (this.listeners.length === 0 && this.interval) {\n        clearInterval(this.interval);\n        this.interval = null;\n      }\n    };\n  }\n\n  private startEmitting() {\n    const events: Array<() => SSEEvent> = [\n      () => ({\n        type: \"release.created\" as SSEEventType,\n        data: {\n          id: `r-live-${Date.now()}`,\n          source: \"dockerhub\",\n          repository: \"library/golang\",\n          raw_version: `1.23.${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10)}`,\n          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n        },\n        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      }),\n      () => ({\n        type: \"pipeline.node_completed\" as SSEEventType,\n        data: {\n          release_id: \"r-004\",\n          node: [\"regex_normalizer\", \"changelog_summarizer\", \"urgency_scorer\"][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)],\n          result: { status: \"ok\" },\n        },\n        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      }),\n      () => ({\n        type: \"pipeline.completed\" as SSEEventType,\n        data: {\n          release_id: \"r-004\",\n          state: \"completed\",\n        },\n        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      }),\n      () => ({\n        type: \"source.polled\" as SSEEventType,\n        data: {\n          source_id: Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 1,\n          repository: [\"library/golang\", \"ethereum/client-go\", \"library/postgres\"][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)],\n          new_releases: Math.floor(Math.random() * 2),\n        },\n        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      }),\n    ];\n\n    this.interval = setInterval(() => {\n      const eventFn = events[Math.floor(Math.random() * events.length)];\n      const event = eventFn();\n      this.listeners.forEach((l) => l(event));\n    }, 5000); // Emit every 5 seconds\n  }\n}\n\nexport const mockSSE = new MockSSE();\n```\n\n**Step 2: Write the activity feed component**\n\n```tsx\n// web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Badge } from \"@/components/ui/badge\";\nimport type { SSEEvent } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\nimport { mockSSE } from \"@/lib/mock/sse\";\n\nconst eventColors: Record<string, string> = {\n  \"release.created\": \"bg-green-100 text-green-800\",\n  \"pipeline.node_completed\": \"bg-blue-100 text-blue-800\",\n  \"pipeline.completed\": \"bg-green-100 text-green-800\",\n  \"pipeline.failed\": \"bg-red-100 text-red-800\",\n  \"source.error\": \"bg-red-100 text-red-800\",\n  \"source.polled\": \"bg-gray-100 text-gray-800\",\n};\n\nfunction formatEvent(event: SSEEvent): string {\n  const d = event.data as Record<string, unknown>;\n  switch (event.type) {\n    case \"release.created\":\n      return `New release: ${d.repository} ${d.raw_version}`;\n    case \"pipeline.node_completed\":\n      return `Pipeline node \"${d.node}\" completed for ${d.release_id}`;\n    case \"pipeline.completed\":\n      return `Pipeline completed for ${d.release_id}`;\n    case \"pipeline.failed\":\n      return `Pipeline failed for ${d.release_id}`;\n    case \"source.polled\":\n      return `Polled ${d.repository} — ${d.new_releases} new`;\n    case \"source.error\":\n      return `Source error: ${d.repository}`;\n    default:\n      return event.type;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function ActivityFeed() {\n  const [events, setEvents] = useState<SSEEvent[]>([]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const unsubscribe = mockSSE.subscribe((event) => {\n      setEvents((prev) => [event, ...prev].slice(0, 20));\n    });\n    return unsubscribe;\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <Card>\n      <CardHeader>\n        <CardTitle className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          Live Activity\n          <span className=\"relative flex h-2 w-2\">\n            <span className=\"absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-green-400 opacity-75\" />\n            <span className=\"relative inline-flex h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-green-500\" />\n          </span>\n        </CardTitle>\n      </CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        {events.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"py-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n            Waiting for events...\n          </p>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"space-y-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto\">\n            {events.map((event, i) => (\n              <div key={`${event.timestamp}-${i}`} className=\"flex items-start gap-2 text-sm\">\n                <Badge className={`shrink-0 text-xs ${eventColors[event.type] ?? \"\"}`}>\n                  {event.type.split(\".\")[1]}\n                </Badge>\n                <span className=\"flex-1 text-muted-foreground\">{formatEvent(event)}</span>\n                <span className=\"shrink-0 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n                  {new Date(event.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}\n                </span>\n              </div>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 3: Add activity feed to dashboard**\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { StatsCards } from \"@/components/dashboard/stats-cards\";\nimport { RecentReleases } from \"@/components/dashboard/recent-releases\";\nimport { ActivityFeed } from \"@/components/dashboard/activity-feed\";\n\nexport default function DashboardPage() {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n      <StatsCards />\n      <div className=\"grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2\">\n        <RecentReleases />\n        <ActivityFeed />\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n**Step 4: Verify live activity feed**\n\nVisit http://localhost:3000 — should see:\n- Stats cards\n- Recent releases (left column)\n- Live activity feed (right column) with green pulsing dot\n- Events appearing every ~5 seconds\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon\ngit add web/lib/mock/sse.ts web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx web/app/page.tsx\ngit commit -m \"feat: add mock SSE emitter and live activity feed on dashboard\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 18: Static Export Verification + Final Polish\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `web/next.config.ts` (if needed)\n- Verify: all pages render correctly\n\n**Step 1: Run TypeScript check**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web && npx tsc --noEmit\n```\n\nExpected: No errors. Fix any type errors.\n\n**Step 2: Run linter**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web && npm run lint\n```\n\nExpected: No errors. Fix any lint issues.\n\n**Step 3: Build static export**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web && npm run build\n```\n\nExpected: Success — `out/` directory created with static HTML files.\n\nNote: If build fails due to `useSearchParams()` in `sources/new/page.tsx`, wrap it in a Suspense boundary or move the search params reading to a client component. Fix:\n\nIf needed, wrap the `NewSourcePage` component:\n```tsx\nimport { Suspense } from \"react\";\n// ... wrap the part using useSearchParams in <Suspense>\n```\n\n**Step 4: Verify static export output**\n\n```bash\nls /Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/out/\n```\n\nExpected: Should contain `index.html`, `projects.html`, `releases.html`, `sources.html`, `subscriptions.html`, `channels.html` (or equivalent directory structure).\n\n**Step 5: Serve static export locally to verify**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web && npx serve out -p 3001\n```\n\nVisit http://localhost:3001 — navigate through all pages. Note: MSW won't work in static mode (it's dev-only), so pages will show loading states. This is expected — the real Go backend will serve the API in production.\n\n**Step 6: Run all tests (if any were added)**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web && npm test 2>/dev/null || echo \"No tests configured yet\"\n```\n\n**Step 7: Final commit**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon\ngit add web/\ngit commit -m \"chore: verify static export and fix any build issues\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n| Task | Description | Key Files |\n|------|------------|-----------|\n| 1 | Scaffold Next.js | `web/` (create-next-app) |\n| 2 | shadcn/ui + SWR + MSW | `web/package.json`, `web/components/ui/` |\n| 3 | TypeScript types | `web/lib/api/types.ts` |\n| 4 | API client | `web/lib/api/client.ts` |\n| 5 | Mock data | `web/lib/mock/data.ts` |\n| 6 | MSW handlers | `web/lib/mock/handlers.ts`, `browser.ts`, `msw-provider.tsx` |\n| 7 | App shell | `web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx`, `header.tsx` |\n| 8 | Dashboard | `web/components/dashboard/`, `web/app/page.tsx` |\n| 9 | Projects list | `web/app/projects/page.tsx` |\n| 10 | Project detail | `web/app/projects/[id]/page.tsx` |\n| 11 | Project forms | `web/components/projects/project-form.tsx`, create/edit pages |\n| 12 | Releases list | `web/app/releases/page.tsx` |\n| 13 | Release detail | `web/components/releases/pipeline-status.tsx`, detail page |\n| 14 | Sources CRUD | `web/components/sources/source-form.tsx`, list/create/edit pages |\n| 15 | Subscriptions CRUD | `web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx`, list/create/edit pages |\n| 16 | Channels CRUD | `web/components/channels/channel-form.tsx`, list/create/edit pages |\n| 17 | SSE + Activity | `web/lib/mock/sse.ts`, `web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx` |\n| 18 | Static export | Verify build, lint, types |\n\n**Total: 18 tasks, ~65 steps**"

     1→# UX Layer + Mocked API Implementation Plan
     2→
     3→> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Build the complete Next.js frontend dashboard with a mocked API layer (MSW), enabling full UI development and iteration without requiring the real Go backend.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** Next.js 15 App Router with static export. MSW (Mock Service Worker) intercepts fetch calls to `/api/v1/*` in development, returning realistic mock data matching the API design spec. When the real Go backend is ready, remove MSW and the same fetch calls hit the real server. SWR handles data fetching, caching, and revalidation.
     8→
     9→**Tech Stack:** Next.js 15, React 19, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS v4, shadcn/ui, SWR, MSW 2.x, Lucide React
    10→
    11→---
    12→
    13→## Directory Structure

---

## Task 2 Complete: Install shadcn/ui and Dependencies

### What was done

**Step 1 -- shadcn/ui initialization:** Ran `npx shadcn@latest init --defaults` which detected Next.js with Tailwind CSS v4, created `components.json` (new-york style, neutral base color, CSS variables enabled), generated `lib/utils.ts` with the `cn()` helper, and updated `app/globals.css` with full CSS variable theming (light + dark mode support).

**Step 2 -- shadcn component installation:** Installed all 15 requested UI components via `npx shadcn@latest add ... --yes --overwrite`. This also pulled in runtime dependencies: `class-variance-authority`, `clsx`, `lucide-react`, `radix-ui`, `tailwind-merge`, and `tw-animate-css`.

**Step 3 -- SWR and MSW:** Installed `swr@^2.4.0` as a production dependency and `msw@^2.12.10` as a dev dependency.

**Step 4 -- MSW service worker:** Ran `npx msw init public/ --save` which created `public/mockServiceWorker.js` and added the `msw.workerDirectory` field to `package.json`.

**Step 5 -- Build verification:** `npm run build` succeeded with no errors.

### Files created

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components.json` | shadcn/ui configuration |
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/lib/utils.ts` | `cn()` class name merge helper |
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/ui/button.tsx` | Button component |
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/ui/card.tsx` | Card component |

to wrap children with MSWProvider.\n\n### handlers.ts\n\n```ts\n// web/lib/mock/handlers.ts\nimport { http, HttpResponse, delay } from \"msw\";\nimport {\n  mockProjects, mockReleases, 100;\nlet nextSubId = 100;\nlet nextChannelId = 100;\n\nfunction envelope<T>(data: T, meta: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {\n  return { data, meta: { request_id: crypto.randomUUID(), ...meta } };\n}\n\nfunction listEnvelope<T>(data: T[], page = 1, perPage = 25) {\n  const start = (page - 1) * perPage;\n  const slice = data.slice(start, start + perPage);\n  return envelope(slice, { page, per_page: perPage, total: data.length });\n}\n\nexport const handlers = [\n  // --- Projects ---\n  http.get(`${BASE}/projects`, async ({ request }) => {\n    await delay(100);\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const page = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"page\") ?? 1);\n    const perPage = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"per_page\") ?? 25);\n    return HttpResponse.json(listEnvelope(mockProjects, page, perPage));\n  }),\n\n  http.post(`${BASE}/projects`, async ({ request }) => {\n    await delay(150);\n    const body = (await request.json()) as Record<string, unknown>;\n    const project = {\n      id: nextProjectId++,\n      ...body,\n      pipeline_config: body.pipeline_config ?? { changelog_summarizer: {}, urgency_scorer: {} },\n HttpResponse.json(envelope(project), { status: 201 });\n  }),\n\n  http.get(`${BASE}/projects/:id`, async ({ params }) => {\n    await delay(80);\n    const project = mockProjects.find((p) => p.id === mockProjects.splice(idx, 1);\n    return HttpResponse.json(envelope(null));\n  }),\n\n  // --- Releases ---\n  http.get(`${BASE}/releases`, async ({ request }) => {\n    await delay(100);\n    const setupWorker } from \"msw/browser\";\nimport { handlers } from \"./handlers\";\n\nexport const worker = setupWorker(...handlers);\n```\n\n### msw-provider.tsx\n\n```tsx\n// web/lib/msw-provider.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from \"react\";\n\nexport function MSWProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== \"development\") {\n      setReady(true);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    import(\"./mock/browser\").then(({ worker }) => {\n      worker.start({ onUnhandledRequest: \"bypass\" }).then(() => setReady(true));\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  if (!ready) return null;\n  return <>{children}</>;\n}\n```\n\n### Update layout.tsx\n\nModify `web/app/layout.tsx` to wrap children with MSWProvider. \"—\", icon: Package, color: \"text-blue-600\" },\n    { label: \"Active Sources\", value: stats?.active_sources ?? \"—\", icon: Radio, color: \"text-green-600\" },\n    { label: \"Pending Jobs\", value: stats?.pending_jobs ?? \"—\", icon: Clock, color: \"text-yellow-600\" },\n    { label: \"Failed Jobs\", value: stats?.failed_jobs ?? \"—\", icon: AlertTriangle, color: \"text-red-600\" },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4\">\n      {items.map((item) => (\n        <Card key={item.label}>\n          <CardHeader className=\"flex flex-row items-center justify-between pb-2\">\n            <CardTitle className=\"text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground\">{item.label}</CardTitle>\n            <item.icon className={`h-4 w-4 ${item.color}`} />\n          </CardHeader>\n          <CardContent>\n            <div className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">{isLoading ? (\n          <div className=\"py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n            {data?.data.map((release) => (\n              <Link\n                key={release.id}\n                href={`/releases/${release.id}`}\n                className=\"flex items-center justify-between rounded-md border p-3 transition-colors hover:bg-muted/50\"\n              >\n                <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n                  <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                    <span className=\"font-medium\">{release.project_name}</span>\n                    <span className=\"font-mono text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n                      {release.raw_version}\n                    </span>\n                    {release.is_pre_release && (\n                      <Badge variant=\"outline\" className=\"text-xs\">pre-release</Badge>\n                    )}\n                  </div>\n                  <div className=\"mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n                    {release.source_type} · {release.repository} ·{\" \"}\n                    {new Date(release.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n                <Badge className={statusColors[release.pipeline_status] ?? All tasks are complete. Here is a summary of what was done:

---

## Summary

### Files Created (7 total)

**Page routes (4 files):**

1. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/app/projects/page.tsx` -- Projects list page with table showing name, description, pipeline nodes, and subscription count. Links to detail and create pages.

2. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/app/projects/[id]/page.tsx` -- Project detail page (server component wrapper). Delegates to `ProjectDetail` client component. Exports `generateStaticParams` with placeholder `[{ id: "0" }]`.

3. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/app/projects/new/page.tsx` -- Create project page using the shared `ProjectForm` component.

4. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/app/projects/[id]/edit/page.tsx` -- Edit project page (server component wrapper). Delegates to `ProjectEdit` client component. Also exports `generateStaticParams` with placeholder.

**Client components (3 files):**

5. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/projects/project-form.tsx` -- Reusable form component for create/edit with fields for name, description, URL, and pipeline config (JSON editor). Handles validation and error display.

6. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/projects/project-detail.tsx` -- Client component for project detail view with pipeline config display, sources list, edit/delete actions, and back navigation.

7. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/projects/project-edit.tsx` -- Client component that fetches project data via SWR and renders `ProjectForm` for editing.

### Architecture Decision: Server/Client Boundary

The original spec had `"use client"` page files with `use(params)` to unwrap the params Promise. However, `output: "export"` in the Next.js config requires `generateStaticParams` on every page under a dynamic `[id]` segment, and `"use client"` pages cannot export server-side functions.

The solution was to split each dynamic route into:
- A **server component page** that exports `generateStaticParams` and `await params`
- A **client component** (in `web/components/projects/`) that contains the actual UI logic with `useSWR`, `useRouter`, etc.

### Next.js Static Export Workaround

A known bug ([vercel/next.js#71862](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/71862)) causes `output: "export"` builds to fail when `generateStaticParams` returns an empty array `[]`. The workaround is returning a placeholder param `[{ id: "0" }]`, which generates a shell page at `/projects/0` and `/projects/0/edit`. Real pages are rendered client-side via SWR after navigation.

### Verification

- `npx tsc --noEmit` -- passes with zero errors
- `npm run build` -- passes, generating all routes correctly:
  - `/projects` (Static)
  - `/projects/[id]` (SSG, generates `/projects/0`)
  - `/projects/[id]/edit` (SSG, generates `/projects/0/edit`)
  - `/projects/new` (Static)

### Commit

4. **`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/releases/pipeline-status.tsx`** -- Pipeline visualization component. Displays completed nodes with their key-value results, active nodes with a spinner, and state-specific icons/badges. Shows attempt count when retries have occurred.
