\"Saving...\" : \"Save\"}</Button>\n          </div>\n        </form>\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### Sources list page\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/sources/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport { sources as sourcesApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Card, CardContent } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Badge } from \"@/components/ui/badge\";\nimport { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from \"@/components/ui/table\";\nimport { Plus } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nexport default function SourcesPage() {\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(\"sources\", () => sourcesApi.list());\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <div>\n          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">All Sources</h2>\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">Ingestion sources that poll upstream registries.</p>\n        </div>\n        <Link href=\"/sources/new\"><Button><Plus className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />Add Source</Button></Link>\n      </div>\n      <Card>\n        <CardContent className=\"p-0\">\n          {isLoading ? (\n            <div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>\n          ) : (\n            <Table>\n              <TableHeader>\n                <TableRow>\n                  <TableHead>Repository</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Type</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Poll Interval</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Last Polled</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Status</TableHead>\n                </TableRow>\n              </TableHeader>\n              <TableBody>\n                {data?.data.map((source) => (\n                  <TableRow key={source.id}>\n                    <TableCell>\n                      <Link href={`/sources/${source.id}/edit`} className=\"font-mono text-sm text-primary hover:underline\">{source.repository}</Link>\n                    </TableCell>\n                    <TableCell><Badge variant=\"outline\">{source.type}</Badge></TableCell>\n                    <TableCell className=\"text-sm\">{source.poll_interval_seconds}s</TableCell>\n                    <TableCell className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">{source.last_polled_at ? new Date(source.last_polled_at).toLocaleString() : \"Never\"}</TableCell>\n                    <TableCell>\n                      {source.last_error ? (\n                        <Badge variant=\"destructive\" className=\"text-xs\">{source.last_error}</Badge>\n                      ) : (\n                        <Badge variant={source.enabled ? \"default\" : \"secondary\"}>{source.enabled ? \"active\" : \"disabled\"}</Badge>\n                      )}\n                    </TableCell>\n                  </TableRow>\n                ))}\n              </TableBody>\n            </Table>\n          )}\n        </CardContent>\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### Sources new page\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/sources/new/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useSearchParams } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { Suspense } from \"react\";\nimport { sources as sourcesApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { SourceForm } from \"@/components/sources/source-form\";\n\nfunction NewSourceContent() {\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const projectId = searchParams.get(\"project_id\");\n  return (\n    <SourceForm\n      title=\"Add Source\"\n      defaultProjectId={projectId ? Number(projectId) : undefined}\n      onSubmit={async (input) => { await sourcesApi.create(input); }}\n    />\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function NewSourcePage() {\n  return (\n    <Suspense fallback={<div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>}>\n      <NewSourceContent />\n    </Suspense>\n  );\n}\n```\n\nNote: `useSearchParams` requires a Suspense boundary for static export.\n\n### source-edit.tsx (client component)\n\n```tsx\n// web/components/sources/source-edit.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { sources as sourcesApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { SourceForm } from \"@/components/sources/source-form\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Trash2 } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nexport function SourceEdit({ id }: { id: string }) {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(`source-${id}`, () => sourcesApi.get(Number(id)));\n\n  const handleDelete = async () => {\n    if (!confirm(\"Delete this source?\")) return;\n    await sourcesApi.delete(Number(id));\n    router.push(\"/sources\");\n  };\n\n  if (isLoading) return <div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>;\n  if (!data?.data) return <div className=\"py-12 text-center\">Source not found</div>;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <SourceForm\n        title=\"Edit Source\"\n        initial={data.data}\n        onSubmit={async (input) => { await sourcesApi.update(Number(id), input); }}\n      />\n      <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl flex justify-end\">\n        <Button variant=\"destructive\" size=\"sm\" onClick={handleDelete}>\n          <Trash2 className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" /> Delete Source\n        </Button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### Sources edit page (server wrapper)\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/sources/[id]/edit/page.tsx\nimport { SourceEdit } from \"@/components/sources/source-edit\";\nexport function generateStaticParams() { return [{ id: \"0\" }]; }\nexport default async function Page({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  const { id } = await params;\n  return <SourceEdit id={id} />;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 15: Subscriptions CRUD\n\n### Files to create:\n\n1. `web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx`\n2. `web/components/subscriptions/subscription-edit.tsx`\n3. `web/app/subscriptions/page.tsx`\n4. `web/app/subscriptions/new/page.tsx`\n5. `web/app/subscriptions/[id]/edit/page.tsx`\n\n### subscription-form.tsx\n\n```tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport { projects as projectsApi, channels as channelsApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Input } from \"@/components/ui/input\";\nimport { Label } from \"@/components/ui/label\";\nimport { Switch } from \"@/components/ui/switch\";\nimport { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from \"@/components/ui/select\";\nimport type { Subscription, SubscriptionInput } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\n\ninterface SubscriptionFormProps {\n  initial?: Subscription;\n  onSubmit: (input: SubscriptionInput) => Promise<void>;\n  title: string;\n}\n\nexport function SubscriptionForm({ initial, onSubmit, title }: SubscriptionFormProps) {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState(\"\");\n  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState(String(initial?.project_id ?? \"\"));\n  const [channelType, setChannelType] = useState(initial?.channel_type ?? \"stable\");\n  const [channelId, setChannelId] = useState(String(initial?.channel_id ?? \"\"));\n  const [versionPattern, setVersionPattern] = useState(initial?.version_pattern ?? \"\");\n  const [frequency, setFrequency] = useState(initial?.frequency ?? \"instant\");\n  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(initial?.enabled ?? true);\n\n  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR(\"projects-for-sub\", () => projectsApi.list());\n  const { data: channelsData } = useSWR(\"channels-for-sub\", () => channelsApi.list());\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError(\"\");\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await onSubmit({\n        project_id: Number(projectId),\n        channel_type: channelType,\n        channel_id: Number(channelId),\n        version_pattern: versionPattern || undefined,\n        frequency: frequency as SubscriptionInput[\"frequency\"],\n        enabled,\n      });\n      router.push(\"/subscriptions\");\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Failed to save\");\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Card className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl\">\n      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n          {error && <div className=\"rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700\">{error}</div>}\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Project</Label>\n            <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={setProjectId} required>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder=\"Select project\" /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (\n                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={String(p.id)}>{p.name}</SelectItem>\n                ))}\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Channel Type</Label>\n            <Select value={channelType} onValueChange={setChannelType}>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n                <SelectItem value=\"stable\">Stable</SelectItem>\n                <SelectItem value=\"pre-release\">Pre-release</SelectItem>\n                <SelectItem value=\"security\">Security</SelectItem>\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Notification Channel</Label>\n            <Select value={channelId} onValueChange={setChannelId} required>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder=\"Select channel\" /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n                {channelsData?.data.map((ch) => (\n                  <SelectItem key={ch.id} value={String(ch.id)}>{ch.name} ({ch.type})</SelectItem>\n                ))}\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"version_pattern\">Version Pattern (regex, optional)</Label>\n            <Input id=\"version_pattern\" value={versionPattern} onChange={(e) => setVersionPattern(e.target.value)} placeholder='e.g. ^v\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+$' />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Frequency</Label>\n            <Select value={frequency} onValueChange={setFrequency}>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n                <SelectItem value=\"instant\">Instant</SelectItem>\n                <SelectItem value=\"hourly\">Hourly Digest</SelectItem>\n                <SelectItem value=\"daily\">Daily Digest</SelectItem>\n                <SelectItem value=\"weekly\">Weekly Digest</SelectItem>\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n            <Switch checked={enabled} onCheckedChange={setEnabled} />\n            <Label>Enabled</Label>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex justify-end gap-2\">\n            <Button type=\"button\" variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => router.back()}>Cancel</Button>\n            <Button type=\"submit\" disabled={saving}>{saving ? \"Saving...\" : \"Save\"}</Button>\n          </div>\n        </form>\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### Subscriptions list page\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/subscriptions/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport { subscriptions as subsApi, projects as projectsApi, channels as channelsApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Card, CardContent } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Badge } from \"@/components/ui/badge\";\nimport { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from \"@/components/ui/table\";\nimport { Plus } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nexport default function SubscriptionsPage() {\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(\"subscriptions\", () => subsApi.list());\n  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR(\"projects-for-sub-list\", () => projectsApi.list());\n  const { data: channelsData } = useSWR(\"channels-for-sub-list\", () => channelsApi.list());\n\n  const getProjectName = (id: number) => projectsData?.data.find((p) => p.id === id)?.name ?? `#${id}`;\n  const getChannelName = (id: number) => channelsData?.data.find((c) => c.id === id)?.name ?? `#${id}`;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <div>\n          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">Subscriptions</h2>\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">Notification routing rules linking projects to channels.</p>\n        </div>\n        <Link href=\"/subscriptions/new\"><Button><Plus className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />New Subscription</Button></Link>\n      </div>\n      <Card>\n        <CardContent className=\"p-0\">\n          {isLoading ? (\n            <div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>\n          ) : (\n            <Table>\n              <TableHeader>\n                <TableRow>\n                  <TableHead>Project</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Type</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Channel</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Frequency</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Pattern</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Status</TableHead>\n                </TableRow>\n              </TableHeader>\n              <TableBody>\n                {data?.data.map((sub) => (\n                  <TableRow key={sub.id}>\n                    <TableCell className=\"font-medium\">{getProjectName(sub.project_id)}</TableCell>\n                    <TableCell><Badge variant=\"outline\">{sub.channel_type}</Badge></TableCell>\n                    <TableCell>{getChannelName(sub.channel_id)}</TableCell>\n                    <TableCell className=\"text-sm\">{sub.frequency}</TableCell>\n                    <TableCell className=\"font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{sub.version_pattern || \"—\"}</TableCell>\n                    <TableCell>\n                      <Link href={`/subscriptions/${sub.id}/edit`}>\n                        <Badge variant={sub.enabled ? \"default\" : \"secondary\"}>{sub.enabled ? \"active\" : \"disabled\"}</Badge>\n                      </Link>\n                    </TableCell>\n                  </TableRow>\n                ))}\n              </TableBody>\n            </Table>\n          )}\n        </CardContent>\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### Subscriptions new page\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/subscriptions/new/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\nimport { subscriptions as subsApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { SubscriptionForm } from \"@/components/subscriptions/subscription-form\";\nexport default function NewSubscriptionPage() {\n  return <SubscriptionForm title=\"Create Subscription\" onSubmit={async (input) => { await subsApi.create(input); }} />;\n}\n```\n\n### subscription-edit.tsx\n\n```tsx\n// web/components/subscriptions/subscription-edit.tsx\n\"use client\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { subscriptions as subsApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { SubscriptionForm } from \"@/components/subscriptions/subscription-form\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Trash2 } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nexport function SubscriptionEdit({ id }: { id: string }) {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(`sub-${id}`, () => subsApi.get(Number(id)));\n  const handleDelete = async () => {\n    if (!confirm(\"Delete this subscription?\")) return;\n    await subsApi.delete(Number(id));\n    router.push(\"/subscriptions\");\n  };\n  if (isLoading) return <div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>;\n  if (!data?.data) return <div className=\"py-12 text-center\">Subscription not found</div>;\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <SubscriptionForm title=\"Edit Subscription\" initial={data.data} onSubmit={async (input) => { await subsApi.update(Number(id), input); }} />\n      <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl flex justify-end\">\n        <Button variant=\"destructive\" size=\"sm\" onClick={handleDelete}><Trash2 className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" /> Delete</Button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### Subscriptions edit page (server wrapper)\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/subscriptions/[id]/edit/page.tsx\nimport { SubscriptionEdit } from \"@/components/subscriptions/subscription-edit\";\nexport function generateStaticParams() { return [{ id: \"0\" }]; }\nexport default async function Page({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  const { id } = await params;\n  return <SubscriptionEdit id={id} />;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 16: Channels CRUD\n\n### Files to create:\n\n1. `web/components/channels/channel-form.tsx`\n2. `web/components/channels/channel-edit.tsx`\n3. `web/app/channels/page.tsx`\n4. `web/app/channels/new/page.tsx`\n5. `web/app/channels/[id]/edit/page.tsx`\n\n### channel-form.tsx\n\n```tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Input } from \"@/components/ui/input\";\nimport { Label } from \"@/components/ui/label\";\nimport { Switch } from \"@/components/ui/switch\";\nimport { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from \"@/components/ui/select\";\nimport type { NotificationChannel, ChannelInput } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\n\nconst channelFields: Record<string, { label: string; placeholder: string }[]> = {\n  slack: [\n    { label: \"Webhook URL\", placeholder: \"https://hooks.slack.com/services/...\" },\n    { label: \"Channel\", placeholder: \"#releases\" },\n  ],\n  pagerduty: [\n    { label: \"Routing Key\", placeholder: \"R0xxxxx\" },\n  ],\n  webhook: [\n    { label: \"URL\", placeholder: \"https://your-service.com/api/releases\" },\n    { label: \"Headers\", placeholder: \"Authorization: Bearer token\" },\n  ],\n};\n\ninterface ChannelFormProps {\n  initial?: NotificationChannel;\n  onSubmit: (input: ChannelInput) => Promise<void>;\n  title: string;\n}\n\nexport function ChannelForm({ initial, onSubmit, title }: ChannelFormProps) {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState(\"\");\n  const [type, setType] = useState(initial?.type ?? \"slack\");\n  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name ?? \"\");\n  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(initial?.enabled ?? true);\n  const [config, setConfig] = useState<Record<string, string>>(initial?.config ?? {});\n\n  const fields = channelFields[type] ?? [];\n\n  const handleConfigChange = (label: string, value: string) => {\n    const key = label.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g, \"_\");\n    setConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));\n  };\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError(\"\");\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await onSubmit({ type, name, config, enabled });\n      router.push(\"/channels\");\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Failed to save\");\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Card className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl\">\n      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n          {error && <div className=\"rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700\">{error}</div>}\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label htmlFor=\"name\">Name</Label>\n            <Input id=\"name\" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder=\"e.g. Engineering Releases\" required />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Type</Label>\n            <Select value={type} onValueChange={(v) => { setType(v); setConfig({}); }}>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n                <SelectItem value=\"slack\">Slack</SelectItem>\n                <SelectItem value=\"pagerduty\">PagerDuty</SelectItem>\n                <SelectItem value=\"webhook\">Webhook</SelectItem>\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          {fields.map((field) => {\n            const key = field.label.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g, \"_\");\n            return (\n              <div key={key} className=\"space-y-2\">\n                <Label htmlFor={key}>{field.label}</Label>\n                <Input id={key} value={config[key] ?? \"\"} onChange={(e) => handleConfigChange(field.label, e.target.value)} placeholder={field.placeholder} />\n              </div>\n            );\n          })}\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n            <Switch checked={enabled} onCheckedChange={setEnabled} />\n            <Label>Enabled</Label>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex justify-end gap-2\">\n            <Button type=\"button\" variant=\"outline\" onClick={() => router.back()}>Cancel</Button>\n            <Button type=\"submit\" disabled={saving}>{saving ? \"Saving...\" : \"Save\"}</Button>\n          </div>\n        </form>\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### Channels list page\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/channels/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport { channels as channelsApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Card, CardContent } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Badge } from \"@/components/ui/badge\";\nimport { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from \"@/components/ui/table\";\nimport { Plus } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nexport default function ChannelsPage() {\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(\"channels\", () => channelsApi.list());\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <div>\n          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold\">Notification Channels</h2>\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">Output targets for release notifications.</p>\n        </div>\n        <Link href=\"/channels/new\"><Button><Plus className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />Add Channel</Button></Link>\n      </div>\n      <Card>\n        <CardContent className=\"p-0\">\n          {isLoading ? (\n            <div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>\n          ) : (\n            <Table>\n              <TableHeader>\n                <TableRow>\n                  <TableHead>Name</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Type</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Config</TableHead>\n                  <TableHead>Status</TableHead>\n                </TableRow>\n              </TableHeader>\n              <TableBody>\n                {data?.data.map((ch) => (\n                  <TableRow key={ch.id}>\n                    <TableCell><Link href={`/channels/${ch.id}/edit`} className=\"font-medium text-primary hover:underline\">{ch.name}</Link></TableCell>\n                    <TableCell><Badge variant=\"outline\">{ch.type}</Badge></TableCell>\n                    <TableCell className=\"max-w-xs truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono\">{Object.entries(ch.config).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join(\", \")}</TableCell>\n                    <TableCell><Badge variant={ch.enabled ? \"default\" : \"secondary\"}>{ch.enabled ? \"active\" : \"disabled\"}</Badge></TableCell>\n                  </TableRow>\n                ))}\n              </TableBody>\n            </Table>\n          )}\n        </CardContent>\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### Channels new page\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/channels/new/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\nimport { channels as channelsApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { ChannelForm } from \"@/components/channels/channel-form\";\nexport default function NewChannelPage() {\n  return <ChannelForm title=\"Add Channel\" onSubmit={async (input) => { await channelsApi.create(input); }} />;\n}\n```\n\n### channel-edit.tsx\n\n```tsx\n// web/components/channels/channel-edit.tsx\n\"use client\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { channels as channelsApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { ChannelForm } from \"@/components/channels/channel-form\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Trash2 } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nexport function ChannelEdit({ id }: { id: string }) {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(`channel-${id}`, () => channelsApi.get(Number(id)));\n  const handleDelete = async () => {\n    if (!confirm(\"Delete this channel?\")) return;\n    await channelsApi.delete(Number(id));\n    router.push(\"/channels\");\n  };\n  if (isLoading) return <div className=\"py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground\">Loading...</div>;\n  if (!data?.data) return <div className=\"py-12 text-center\">Channel not found</div>;\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <ChannelForm title=\"Edit Channel\" initial={data.data} onSubmit={async (input) => { await channelsApi.update(Number(id), input); }} />\n      <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-2xl flex justify-end\">\n        <Button variant=\"destructive\" size=\"sm\" onClick={handleDelete}><Trash2 className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" /> Delete</Button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n### Channels edit page (server wrapper)\n\n```tsx\n// web/app/channels/[id]/edit/page.tsx\nimport { ChannelEdit } from \"@/components/channels/channel-edit\";\nexport function generateStaticParams() { return [{ id: \"0\" }];   3344→## Task 17: SSE Mock + Live Activity Feed
  3345→
  3346→**Files:**
  3347→- Create: `web/lib/mock/sse.ts`
  3348→- Create: `web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx`
  3349→- Modify: `web/app/page.tsx`
  3350→
  3351→MSW doesn't intercept SSE connections natively. Instead, we create a mock SSE emitter that simulates real-time events in development.
  3352→
  3353→**Step 1: Write the mock SSE emitter**
  3354→
  3355→```ts
  3356→// web/lib/mock/sse.ts
  3357→import type { SSEEvent, SSEEventType } from "../api/types";
  3358→
  3359→type SSEListener = (event: SSEEvent) => void;
  3360→
  3361→class MockSSE {
  3362→  private listeners: SSEListener[] = [];
  3363→  private interval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  3364→
  3365→  subscribe(listener: SSEListener) {
  3366→    this.listeners.push(listener);
  3367→    if (!this.interval) this.startEmitting();
  3368→    return () => {
  3369→      this.listeners = this.listeners.filter((l) => l !== listener);
  3370→      if (this.listeners.length === 0 && this.interval) {
  3371→        clearInterval(this.interval);
  3372→        this.interval = null;
  3373→      }
  3374→    };
  3375→  }
  3376→
  3377→  private startEmitting() {
  3378→    const events: Array<() => SSEEvent> = [
  3379→      () => ({
  3380→        type: "release.created" as SSEEventType,
  3381→        data: {
  3382→          id: `r-live-${Date.now()}`,
  3383→          source: "dockerhub",
  3384→          repository: "library/golang",
  3385→          raw_version: `1.23.${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10)}`,
  3386→          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  3387→        },
  3388→        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  3389→      }),
  3390→      () => ({
  3391→        type: "pipeline.node_completed" as SSEEventType,
  3392→        data: {
  3393→          release_id: "r-004",
  3394→          node: ["regex_normalizer", "changelog_summarizer", "urgency_scorer"][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)],
  3395→          result: { status: "ok" },
  3396→        },
  3397→        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  3398→      }),
  3399→      () => ({
  3400→        type: "pipeline.completed" as SSEEventType,
  3401→        data: {
  3402→          release_id: "r-004",
  3403→          state: "completed",
  3404→        },
  3405→        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  3406→      }),
  3407→      () => ({
  3408→        type: "source.polled" as SSEEventType,
  3409→        data: {
  3410→          source_id: Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 1,
  3411→          repository: ["library/golang", "ethereum/client-go", "library/postgres"][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)],
  3412→          new_releases: Math.floor(Math.random() * 2),
  3413→        },
  3414→        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  3415→      }),
  3416→    ];
  3417→
  3418→    this.interval = setInterval(() => {
  3419→      const eventFn = events[Math.floor(Math.random() * events.length)];
  3420→      const event = eventFn();
  3421→      this.listeners.forEach((l) => l(event));
  3422→    }, 5000); // Emit every 5 seconds
  3423→  }
  3424→}
  3425→
  3426→export const mockSSE = new MockSSE();
  3427→```
  3428→
  3429→**Step 2: Write the activity feed component**
  3430→
  3431→```tsx
  3432→// web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx
  3433→"use client";
  3434→
  3435→import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
  3436→import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
  3437→import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
  3438→import type { SSEEvent } from "@/lib/api/types";
  3439→import { mockSSE } from "@/lib/mock/sse";
  3440→
  3441→const eventColors: Record<string, string> = {
  3442→  "release.created": "bg-green-100 text-green-800",
  3443→  "pipeline.node_completed": "bg-blue-100 text-blue-800",
  3444→  "pipeline.completed": "bg-green-100 text-green-800",
  3445→  "pipeline.failed": "bg-red-100 text-red-800",
  3446→  "source.error": "bg-red-100 text-red-800",
  3447→  "source.polled": "bg-gray-100 text-gray-800",
  3448→};
  3449→
  3450→function formatEvent(event: SSEEvent): string {
  3451→  const d = event.data as Record<string, unknown>;
  3452→  switch (event.type) {
  3453→    case "release.created":
  3454→      return `New release: ${d.repository} ${d.raw_version}`;
  3455→    case "pipeline.node_completed":
  3456→      return `Pipeline node "${d.node}" completed for ${d.release_id}`;
  3457→    case "pipeline.completed":
  3458→      return `Pipeline completed for ${d.release_id}`;
  3459→    case "pipeline.failed":
  3460→      return `Pipeline failed for ${d.release_id}`;
  3461→    case "source.polled":
  3462→      return `Polled ${d.repository} — ${d.new_releases} new`;
  3463→    case "source.error":
  3464→      return `Source error: ${d.repository}`;
  3465→    default:
  3466→      return event.type;
  3467→  }
  3468→}
  3469→
  3470→export function ActivityFeed() {
  3471→  const [events, setEvents] = useState<SSEEvent[]>([]);
  3472→
  3473→  useEffect(() => {
  3474→    const unsubscribe = mockSSE.subscribe((event) => {
  3475→      setEvents((prev) => [event, ...prev].slice(0, 20));
  3476→    });
  3477→    return unsubscribe;
  3478→  }, []);
  3479→
  3480→  return (
  3481→    <Card>
  3482→      <CardHeader>
  3483→        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
  3484→          Live Activity
  3485→          <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
  3486→            <span className="absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-green-400 opacity-75" />
  3487→            <span className="relative inline-flex h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-green-500" />
  3488→          </span>
  3489→        </CardTitle>
  3490→      </CardHeader>
  3491→      <CardContent>
  3492→        {events.length === 0 ? (
  3493→          <p className="py-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
  3494→            Waiting for events...
  3495→          </p>
  3496→        ) : (
  3497→          <div className="space-y-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto">
  3498→            {events.map((event, i) => (
  3499→              <div key={`${event.timestamp}-${i}`} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm">
  3500→                <Badge className={`shrink-0 text-xs ${eventColors[event.type] ?? ""}`}>
  3501→                  {event.type.split(".")[1]}
  3502→                </Badge>
  3503→                <span className="flex-1 text-muted-foreground">{formatEvent(event)}</span>
  3504→                <span className="shrink-0 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
  3505→                  {new Date(event.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}
  3506→                </span>
  3507→              </div>
  3508→            ))}
  3509→          </div>
  3510→        )}
  3511→      </CardContent>
  3512→    </Card>
  3513→  );
  3514→}
  3515→```

Date().toISOString(),\n      }),\n    ];\n\n    this.interval = setInterval(() => {\n      const eventFn = events[Math.floor(Math.random() * events.length)];\n      const event = eventFn();\n      this.listeners.forEach((l) => l(event));\n    }, 5000); // Emit every 5 seconds\n  }\n}\n\nexport const mockSSE = new MockSSE();\n```\n\n### Step 2: Create ## Self-Review Summary

### Task 17: SSE Mock + Live Activity Feed -- DONE

**Commit:** `02acb69` -- `feat: add mock SSE emitter and live activity feed on dashboard`

Files created/modified:

1. **`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/lib/mock/sse.ts`** -- `MockSSE` class with a clean subscribe/unsubscribe pattern. The class emits random SSE events (`release.created`, `pipeline.node_completed`, `pipeline.completed`, `source.polled`) every 5 seconds. Uses proper TypeScript types from `SSEEvent`/`SSEEventType`. The interval auto-starts on first subscriber and auto-stops when the last subscriber unsubscribes (no memory leaks).

2. **`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx`** -- `ActivityFeed` component that subscribes to the mock SSE emitter via `useEffect` with proper cleanup. Displays up to 20 events in reverse chronological order. Each event shows a colored `Badge` (green for releases/pipeline completed, blue for pipeline node progress, gray for polling, red for errors), a human-readable description, and a timestamp. Includes a pulsing green dot in the header to indicate "live" status. Scrollable container with `max-h-80`.

3. **`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/app/page.tsx`** -- Updated dashboard to use a 2-column grid (`lg:grid-cols-2`) with `RecentReleases` on the left and `ActivityFeed` on the right, below the stats cards.

- **Static export (`npm run build`):** Succeeds, all 19 pages generated in `out/` directory including the updated `index.html` dashboard

---

## Code Review: Tasks 17-18 (commits `02acb69` and `e3a7be9`)

Files reviewed:
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/lib/mock/sse.ts`
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx`
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/app/page.tsx`
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/lib/mock/handlers.ts`
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/eslint.config.mjs`

---

### Strengths

- **Correct SSE cleanup lifecycle.** `MockSSE.subscribe` returns an unsubscribe closure that both removes the listener from the array and, when the array empties, calls `clearInterval` and nulls the handle. The interval is therefore never left dangling after the last subscriber is gone.

- **Correct React effect cleanup.** `ActivityFeed` returns the unsubscribe function directly from `useEffect` with an empty dep array (`[]`). This is the idiomatic pattern — no wrapper function, no closure capturing stale state, cleanup fires exactly once on unmount.

- **State update is safe across concurrent renders.** `setEvents((prev) => [event, ...prev].slice(0, 20))` uses the functional form, which is correct; it does not close over the `events` value from the render scope.

- **20-item hard cap.** The `.slice(0, 20)` bounds the state array, so there is no unbounded memory growth from long-lived subscriptions.

- **`SSEEventType` is a closed union.** The type definition in `types.ts` is a string literal union, not a plain `string`. The `as SSEEventType` casts in `sse.ts` are in mock/test code only and are acceptable there.

- **`formatEvent` handles all enum members.** Every member of `SSEEventType` has a case in the switch, plus a `default` fallback. No silent no-ops.

- **Interval is started lazily and only once.** The `if (!this.interval) this.startEmitting()` guard prevents double-interval creation when a second subscriber attaches.

- **ESLint config is minimal and correct.** The override adds `public/mockServiceWorker.js` to the ignore list without touching any lint rules. The comment clearly explains why.

- **`handlers.ts` lint fixes are clean.** The module-level mutable counters (`nextProjectId`, etc.) and the `as unknown as T` cast pattern are consistent and appropriately scoped to mock infrastructure.

---

### Issues

#### Critical

None.

---

#### Important

**1. Composite key using array index is fragile (`activity-feed.tsx`, line 68)**

```tsx
key={`${event.timestamp}-${i}`}
```

The `timestamp` field from `sse.ts` is `new Date().toISOString()` — millisecond resolution. Two events emitted within the same millisecond (theoretically possible if `setInterval` fires while the JS engine is under load, or in tests) will produce duplicate keys. The array index `i` is appended as a tie-breaker, but `i` shifts as new events are prepended (`[event, ...prev]`), meaning every existing row gets a new key on each update. React will reuse DOM nodes correctly because keys still differ within a single render, but using a shifting index defeats the purpose of stable keys for reconciliation — existing items will be unnecessarily re-evaluated as if they moved.

The correct fix is to attach a stable unique id at the moment of emission in `sse.ts` and use that as the key:

```ts
// sse.ts — add id to the SSEEvent or wrap it
const id = `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
```

```tsx
// activity-feed.tsx
key={event.id}  // stable, never shifts
```

Alternatively, assign the id inside the `subscribe` callback before calling `setEvents`. Either way, keys must be stable across re-renders for the reconciler to work correctly.

---

**2. `SSEEvent.data` is typed `unknown`, requiring an unsafe cast in `formatEvent` (`activity-feed.tsx`, line 20)**

```ts
const d = event.data as Record<string, unknown>;
```

This cast silences the type checker. If a future event variant has a `data` shape that does not include `repository` or `release_id`, the rendered string will show `"undefined"` with no compile-time warning.

The idiomatic fix is to make `SSEEvent` a discriminated union:

```ts
// types.ts
export type SSEEvent =
  | { type: "release.created"; data: { id: string; repository: string; raw_version: string; source: string; created_at: string }; timestamp: string }
  | { type: "pipeline.node_completed"; data: { release_id: string; node: string; result: unknown }; timestamp: string }
  | { type: "pipeline.completed"; data: { release_id: string; state: string }; timestamp: string }
  | { type: "pipeline.failed"; data: { release_id: string }; timestamp: string }
  | { type: "source.polled"; data: { source_id: number; repository: string; new_releases: number }; timestamp: string }
  | { type: "source.error"; data: { repository: string }; timestamp: string };
```

With that shape, `formatEvent`'s switch cases narrow `event.data` automatically and the `as Record<string, unknown>` cast can be deleted entirely. This is worth doing now rather than later — the type boundary is small and the mock already defines all the shapes.

---

#### Minor

**3. `startEmitting` creates the `events` factory array on every call (`sse.ts`, line 23)**

```ts
private startEmitting() {
  const events: Array<() => SSEEvent> = [ ... ];
  this.interval = setInterval(() => { ... }, 5000);
}
```

`startEmitting` is only ever called once (guarded by `if (!this.interval)`), so this is not a real-world performance problem. However, if that guard were ever bypassed, the array would be re-allocated. Moving `events` to a class-level `private readonly` field or to a module-level constant would be marginally cleaner and makes the intent clearer.

---

**4. `page.tsx` is marked `"use client"` unnecessarily**

```tsx
// web/app/page.tsx
"use client";
```

`DashboardPage` itself contains no hooks and no client-side state. It renders three child components: `StatsCards`, `RecentReleases`, and `ActivityFeed`. The `"use client"` directive should live at the leaf that actually needs it (`ActivityFeed` already has it). Marking the page-level layout component as a client component forces the entire subtree — including `StatsCards` and `RecentReleases` — to be bundled as client JavaScript even if those components could be server components. This is a minor bundle-size concern in the current mock context, but it is the wrong pattern to establish for a dashboard that will eventually have server-rendered data.

---

**5. `event.type.split(".")[1]` can produce `undefined` for future event types (`activity-feed.tsx`, line 70)**

```tsx
{event.type.split(".")[1]}
```

If any `SSEEventType` value is ever added that has no dot (e.g. a top-level `"heartbeat"` type), `split(".")[1]` returns `undefined` and React renders nothing. A safer alternative is `event.type.split(".").at(-1)` or an explicit lookup map for display labels.

---

### Verdict: NEEDS_CHANGES

Two important issues block approval. The shifting-index key problem (issue 1) means React cannot perform stable reconciliation on the live feed — on every new event, all 20 existing rows are keyed differently than they were in the previous render. The `unknown` cast pattern (issue 2) should be fixed now while the discriminated union is easy to add; it will only get harder as more components consume `SSEEvent`. The minor issues (3–5) can accompany those fixes in the same pass.

  "content": "// web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from \"@/components/ui/card\";\nimport { Badge } from \"@/components/ui/badge\";\nimport type { SSEEvent } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\nimport { mockSSE } from \"@/lib/mock/sse\";\n\nconst eventColors: Record<string, string> = {\n  \"release.created\": \"bg-green-100 text-green-800\",\n  \"pipeline.node_completed\": \"bg-blue-100 text-blue-800\",\n  \"pipeline.completed\": \"bg-green-100 text-green-800\",\n  \"pipeline.failed\": \"bg-red-100 text-red-800\",\n  \"source.error\": \"bg-red-100 text-red-800\",\n  \"source.polled\": \"bg-gray-100 text-gray-800\",\n};\n\nfunction formatEvent(event: SSEEvent): string {\n  switch (event.type) {\n    case \"release.created\":\n      return `New release: ${event.data.repository} ${event.data.raw_version}`;\n    case \"pipeline.node_completed\":\n      return `Pipeline node \"${event.data.node}\" completed for ${event.data.release_id}`;\n    case \"pipeline.completed\":\n      return `Pipeline completed for ${event.data.release_id}`;\n    case \"pipeline.failed\":\n      return `Pipeline failed for ${event.data.release_id}`;\n    case \"source.polled\":\n      return `Polled ${event.data.repository} — ${event.data.new_releases} new`;\n    case \"source.error\":\n      return `Source error: ${event.data.repository}`;\n    default:\n      return event satisfies never;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction eventLabel(type: SSEEvent[\"type\"]): string {\n  const idx = type.indexOf(\".\");\n  return idx >= 0 ? type.slice(idx + 1) : type;\n}\n\nexport function ActivityFeed() {\n  const [events, setEvents] = useState<SSEEvent[]>([]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const unsubscribe = mockSSE.subscribe((event) => {\n      setEvents((prev) => [event, ...prev].slice(0, 20));\n    });\n    return unsubscribe;\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <Card>\n      <CardHeader>\n        <CardTitle className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          Live Activity\n          <span className=\"relative flex h-2 w-2\">\n            <span className=\"absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-green-400 opacity-75\" />\n            <span className=\"relative inline-flex h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-green-500\" />\n          </span>\n        </CardTitle>\n      </CardHeader>\n      <CardContent>\n        {events.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"py-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n            Waiting for events...\n          </p>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"space-y-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto\">\n            {events.map((event) => (\n              <div key={event.id} className=\"flex items-start gap-2 text-sm\">\n                <Badge className={`shrink-0 text-xs ${eventColors[event.type] ?? \"\"}`}>\n                  {eventLabel(event.type)}\n                </Badge>\n                <span className=\"flex-1 text-muted-foreground\">{formatEvent(event)}</span>\n                <span className=\"shrink-0 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n                  {new Date(event.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}\n                </span>\n              </div>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n"
}

tool_result
id: toolu_0183cGC86qTuYo1aVdwsYYpr
```
**APPROVED**

Both original issues are correctly resolved. Here is a detailed breakdown per file.

---

**Issue 1 — Unstable React keys**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx`, line 72.

The old `key={event.timestamp-${i}}` embedded the array index `i`, meaning React would see the same key for a different element whenever items shifted in the prepend-and-slice pattern used on line 48. The fix uses `key={event.id}` — a value that is stable and unique to each event object for its entire lifetime in the list. This is correct.

---

**Issue 2 — Unsafe `unknown` cast**

Previously `event.data as Record<string, unknown>` bypassed the type system entirely. The fix is thorough and goes further than a minimal patch:

`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/lib/api/types.ts` (lines 167–196): `SSEEvent` is now a proper discriminated union. Each variant carries `type` as a string literal and a `data` shape that is specific and narrowly typed to that variant. The shared fields (`id`, `timestamp`) are factored into `SSEBase` and intersected into each member. The `id: string` field requested in the brief is present on `SSEBase` and therefore on every variant.

`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/lib/mock/sse.ts` (lines 6–9): A module-level `counter` combined with `Date.now()` produces IDs of the form `sse-<timestamp>-<monotonic-counter>`. This is collision-resistant across rapid successive calls (the counter prevents duplicates within the same millisecond) and is stable for the lifetime of the event object. Each factory call on lines 30, 42, 51, and 63 invokes `uid()` independently, so every emitted event gets a distinct ID.

`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/.worktrees/ux-layer/web/components/dashboard/activity-feed.tsx` (lines 19–36): The `formatEvent` function is a `switch` on `event.type`. Inside each `case` branch TypeScript narrows `event` to the exact variant, so `event.data.repository`, `event.data.node`, `event.data.release_id`, etc. are all accessed without any cast. The exhaustiveness check on line 34 (`return event satisfies never`) ensures a compile-time error if a new variant is added to the union without updating this switch — a meaningful safety improvement over the original. There is no `as` cast anywhere in the file.

---

**No remaining concerns.** The discriminated union design, the stable ID generation strategy, and the exhaustive narrowing pattern are all production-quality. Nothing in these three files requires further changes.
